Started 2026-10-05; revised 2026-10-11 after the [reliability audit](ROBUSTNESS.md).
This ledger describes **unchanged G17**, Q = 384, U = 20,736, λ = 24,
c = 16. G16 evidence is labeled as such and does not automatically transfer.
The active rule, recipes, manifest and compiler have not been modified.

J309/G285. No complete iid reliability theorem or smallest globally
sufficient change is established. New individual replays/preflight passed;
the complete post-J309 audit did not execute after automatic approval
review enforced the stop instruction. The previous full audit covers
J307. [ROBUSTNESS_HANDOFF.md](ROBUSTNESS_HANDOFF.md) records receipts,
open obligations and the unaccepted GBD7 draft.

- **proved**: the stated result, with its stated hypotheses, has a proof in
  ROBUSTNESS.md or a cited argument that applies. Arithmetic and static layout
  facts do not prove dynamical repair.
- **argued and measured**: an incomplete argument and finite experimental
  support; **unproved** for a reliability theorem.
- **measured only**: finite experimental support without a complete argument;
  **unproved** for a reliability theorem.
- **assumed**: a required but unproved implication without universal verification.
  A false unrestricted version is explicitly identified, not retained as a fact.

The requested all-time memory/noise theorem is **not established**. The audit
states it precisely, proves the error-set part, the universal abstract program,
noiseless simulation of every upper state from complete encodings, their
E0-only tolerance and restoration from arbitrary rest fields after physical
alignment, pristine hierarchy closure and its operational level-0
correction, and a
conditional final probability calculation. It identifies the missing
deterministic recovery lemmas. No smallest change
sufficient for the full theorem has been proved. A concrete out-of-range ROM
fetch defect is also present in the current executables; its narrow remedy is
justified below. None of these findings is a proof that the mathematically
completed G17 Boolean rule cannot be reliable.

## A. The error classes (Gray §5.1)

| ID | Statement | Status | Where / limit |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | For Q,U >= 6(λ+2), every point above level n−1 is in a candidate level-n set of side at most (λ+2) at scale n−1, for any c >= 1 | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §3 supplies the complete induction, including infinite error sets. The candidate need not have exactly 2^n points or have the prescribed point in its dense witness. |
| A2 | A real level-n error fits in a (c+2λ+4)-box at scale n−1: **68** for G17. At level 1 the sharp width/duration bound is **62** | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §3. The often quoted **70** upper-cell footprint is a dynamical allowance of two further cells, not part of this lemma. |
| A3 | If e_n is the probability a Q^n × U^n box meets a candidate level-n error, e_n <= (Q²U²ε)^(2^n) for G17 | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §4 uses monotone raw witnesses and the same BK inequality as Gray. It does not assert the old recurrence e_n <= (QU e_(n−1))² follows verbatim for nonmonotone actual candidates. |
| A4 | Every error has finite level almost surely when ε < 1/(3249 QU); in particular when ε < (QU)^−2 | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §4, equations (1)–(4). This concerns error locations, not whether the rule repairs them. |
| A5 | Distinct real level-n errors, and a level-n error and any higher error, are (24Q^n,24U^n)-separated | **proved** | Definition (iv), with the overlap argument in ROBUSTNESS §3. |

For G17, (QU)^−2 = **1.5772029579 × 10^−14**. The audit also proves the
less conservative error-set sufficient condition ε < **3.8653967761 × 10^−11**.
**Neither is an established noise threshold of the rule.** The conditional
memory number in ROBUSTNESS §11 also depends on an unproved repair lemma.
The former ledger's rounded 1.8 × 10^−14 and its unconditional import of
Gray's recurrence are superseded.

## B. The rule against level-0 errors

| ID | Statement | Status | Evidence / missing hypothesis |
|---|---|---|---|
| B0 | Five agreeing copies on five distinct holders retain their correct majority after two holders are altered | **proved** | Elementary vote, ROBUSTNESS §5; complete encoded copy/clock invariants and E0-only tolerance are proved in §6. Extension to arbitrary partial/damaged regions is unproved. |
| B1 | On the pristine noiseless hierarchy orbit, arbitrary one-site or adjacent-pair overwrites disappear in one error-free update | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §6, equation (7), covers every tick and replacement word. Existing 6,000 random and 6,000 inverted G17 healthy trials remain additional evidence. No unrestricted flagged-region claim is made. |
| B2 | Two level-0 errors tolerate the reduced spacing needed for upper footprints | **measured only** | G16: 14,784 tested pairs, including same-tick pairs with at least 9 clean sites between them; ERROR_LEVELS §2.2. Not a universal correction lemma for G17. |
| B2a | On the pristine orbit, (7) holds for every overwrite set with at most two sites in any eleven-site interval, at every tick | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §6; includes adjacent pairs with nine clean sites between pairs. It does not prove the damaged-trajectory/flagged-region extension needed by B2 and D2. |
| B2b | Aligned Address and common Age correct before each next overwrite when each five-site interval has at most two current faults, regardless of all flags and simulation fields | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §10: both Address and both Age votes have three correct whole words, so either flag-selected vote is correct. No temporal separation is needed. This proves geometry-only closure from aligned structure, not all-field correction or arbitrary damaged upper decoding. |
| B3 | A raw Address hit in a flagged region can leave a two-tick simulation-field footprint | **proved** for the explicit legal flagged state; also measured on running states | ROBUSTNESS §5 and `figs/robustness/checks.json`: five Info bits wrong at tick 1, exact at tick 2, C/NumPy agree. This disproves unrestricted one-tick recovery, not memory from a pristine hierarchy. |
| B3a | Differences in the Flag2 return wave have a bounded, absorbable footprint | **assumed** | The existing G17 upper-wipe receipt has 3/200 post-window differences still present after three ticks. Their universal lifetime/envelope and absorption into a larger island are unproved; ROBUSTNESS §§5,8–10. |
| B4 | Complete encodings of arbitrary upper states tolerate all mutually separated E0 overwrite patterns and reset to Enc_a at every period boundary outside current faults | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §6, (5b): simulation-copy errors are confined to N_t union N_(t−1), early mail is protected, later mail is unused, and both flags quiet before commit. Decoding at every boundary is exactly F^n(z). This covers E0-only full encodings, not partial colonies or E0 interacting with higher-error damage. Finite support: `figs/robustness/encoded_e0.json`. |

B2 is not sufficient to justify D2 until both a G17 correction invariant and
an effective-error map have been proved. Delayed errors inside Flag1 regions
may be harmless within higher damage boxes, but that inclusion must be proved.

## C. The rule against level-1 errors

| ID | Statement | Status | Evidence / limit |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | A level-1 damage island is erased and confined to the required two-colony envelope amid lower-level noise | **argued and measured**; universal statement unproved | Gray's Q > (92/3)B hypothesis fails, even for B=62. REPORT §32 replaces it by a patterned race, not an arbitrary-noise domination proof; ROBUSTNESS §7. A new 512-case one-tick legal flag/phase seed probe has exact structural equality at tick 4096, but 272 cases still have simulation-field residues; no E0 noise, finite slice and sixteen-tick samples (ROBUSTNESS §13). |
| C1a | For the tested held shifted-block pattern, survival thresholds agree with (5/6)(Q−w) | **argued and measured** | Five G16 and three G17 brackets, REPORT §§32–33; `figs/level0/G17_misaligned_block.json`. Arbitrary flags, Ages, invalid words, mixed lower noise and different islands are not covered. |
| C1b | 62 and the 73-cell design width are below the no-noise square-block race estimate (5/11)Q ≈ 174.5 | **proved**, arithmetic only | Does not establish that all level-1 errors are below a universal survival threshold. The safety inference formerly attached to this comparison is unproved. |
| C1c | Same-tick fault triples of span at most ten inside one real c=16 level-1 error fit in a 34-site by 16-tick box | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §7, equation (8). With only this S and E0 noise, all non-direct damage starts in its forward causal cone; with no such triple all faults correct by (7). This localizes initial forcing, but does not bound erosion or later perturbations of an island. |
| C1d | Until one tick after the last such triple, indirect damage from a single level-1 error and E0 noise spans at most 194 sites | **proved** | The cone in C1c has source span 33 and at most sixteen radius-5 steps: 33+160=193, or 194 sites. It stays below Q−10 during this forcing interval. Subsequent island growth and erosion are unproved. |
| C1e | Each Age follows whole-word ancestry; every unused clock word becomes legal within 16,352 increments | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §7, (C1)–(C3), and exhaustive `Report/proofs/check_g17_clock.py`. From legal initial clocks the probability of an illegal Age is at most 1,336,874,112 ε at each site/time. This says nothing about correct phase or hierarchy reliability. Natural normalization is too slow to supply the desired short recovery bound. |
| C1f | No new Address phase modulo Q is created without an Address overwrite; canonical Address words form a closed domain | **proved** | Whole-word selection and the exact adder's residue identity, ROBUSTNESS §7. Incorrect phases can persist/spread; a globally constant invalid Address field is invariant, so Address legality has no unconditional time-normalization theorem. |
| C1g | A geometric damage interval amid E0 noise grows by at most (75/23)T+2 at either edge in T updates, allowing arbitrary flags and other fields | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §7, (G1)–(G3); permits additional forcing inside the interval. Necessary three-bad-input condition and separated-group packing, with symbolic/local certificate `Report/proofs/check_geometry_growth.py`. A single legal E0 pair can produce a five-site one-update advance; C/NumPy receipt `figs/robustness/geometry_jump.json`. No erosion/lifetime bound follows. |
| C1h | Sixteen ticks after the first geometry-dangerous triple of one real level-1 error, indirect Address/Age damage fits in 141 sites and all future overwrites have E0 spacing | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §7, (G4): a closest candidate pair determines a <=33-site fixed forcing interval; exterior S points are separated by condition (iii), so C1g bounds the seed. Future S points are also separated after the sixteen-tick cutoff. This is geometry-only and does not reassign S's original error level, prove erosion, or confine all simulation-field damage. |
| C1i | Correct Address phase normalizes to canonical words within 43 Address-error-free updates, regardless of every other field | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §7: every adjusted left word is canonical; an aliased output needs three aliased right inputs. The maximal 128-site alias strip shrinks three sites per update. `Report/proofs/check_address_aliases.py` checks all 5,120 raw word/constant combinations and the domination strip; receipt `figs/robustness/address_alias_certificate.json`. This does not normalize arbitrary phases or prove phase erosion. |
| C1j | Every raw Age word has a legal-cycle phase; no new clock phase is created without an Age overwrite | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §7, (C4): psi(h(a))=psi(a)+1 mod U, with whole-word ancestry. At most four raw words represent one phase. The extended `Report/proofs/check_g17_clock.py` verifies all 65,536 successor identities. Phase provenance does not establish erosion, a recovery deadline or reliability. |

Neutral right-phase aliases are not claimed canonical. `Report/proofs/check_left_boundary.py` verifies 1,966,080 mask cases, 840 actual-gate symbolic cases and sharp local exceptions. Moving-front Flag2 control and escape from apparent boundaries remain unproved. |

It is not a noisy recovery/lifetime theorem and excludes neither Flag2 errors nor leftward escape. |

C/CUDA whole outputs agree; `figs/robustness/quiet_ancestry_burst.npz` and its certificate. This is a rejected proof inference, not a required repository assumption or a disproof of reliability/burst recovery. Independent-vote mixing must be handled explicitly. |
| C1p | A healthy suffix from a true colony boundary resists arbitrary left damage when the first colony has quiet Flag1 and no Workspace.Flag1 sources apart from direct faults | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §7, (G6). Only two faults per eleven-site interval per tick are needed, with no temporal separation. Flags/work fields beyond the first colony and all Flag2 values may be arbitrary. A three-source boundary snapshot shows the Workspace premise cannot simply be dropped. `Report/proofs/check_right_barrier.py`; `figs/robustness/right_barrier_certificate.json`. Existence and source exclusion for an actual damaged-hierarchy boundary remain unproved. |
| C1q | Under the specified healthy suffix/end-supported initial flags, arbitrary command bits still allow 113 full-rule updates of right-barrier protection amid E0 | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §7, (G7): fresh Workspace.Flag1 is end-gated; its source cannot occur before Address Q−10. The Flag1 envelope loses at most `3T+2[1+floor((3T+1)/23)]` sites. At T=113 its front is at five, which closes geometric protection and the end-gate induction. The clock-gated corollary discharges source exclusion during inactive Workspace intervals. No optimal barrier lifetime, arbitrary-seed recovery or sufficient positive-noise theorem follows. |
| C1r | A single coherent wrong Address-phase block of width <=62, with common initial Age zero, repairs G17 geometry permanently within 588 updates | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §12, (J15). Initial Flag2/Workspace bits zero everywhere; Flag1 arbitrary inside the block and zero outside; all other fields arbitrary; no later overwrites. Two-colour majority reduction, true-colony barrier, quiet-set retreat and at most two boundary startups give the complete bound. Flag1 is zero at update 800, before the Workspace stage. `Report/proofs/check_sparse_phase_repair.py`; `figs/robustness/SP2_coherent_phase_certificate.json` contains unchanged G17's local certificate and identities. Arbitrary clocks/multiple phases/Flag2, continuing E0, simulation restoration and a general real level-1 error are not covered. |

Calling a shifted colony “level-2 damage” is a proposed envelope assignment.
It does not certify the original fault set's Gray level or the induction.

Assumes correct gathered inputs, transport and external instruction. |
| D1b | On healthy upper structure, F^U(Enc(z))=Enc(F(z)); the pristine infinite hierarchy closes for all periods | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §6, equations (5)–(6), gives the physical copy/clock, gather, courier, match, wipe and commit induction and the finite-time induction for all levels. D1c extends noiseless simulation to every represented upper state with an augmented encoding; damaged physical continuations remain unproved. |
| D1c | Every upper state, including arbitrary raw geometry/flags and inconsistent upper copies, simulates noiselessly for all periods from a complete augmented boundary encoding | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §6, (5a): Enc_a adds unused Info 58=upper Flag2 and 325=upper Flag1. Both ordinary Enc and Enc_a transition to Enc_a(F(z)); all lower flags/work are clear at the boundary. All-raw-word arrival uniqueness resolves unfetched instructions. This changes the proof encoding, not the rule or codec, and uses the unique output completion. Finite support: `figs/robustness/noiseless_arbitrary.json` and `noiseless_flags_canonical.json`. Physical noise, partial encodings and damaged lower continuations remain unproved. |
| D1d | At most one of the five compact-front logical slots arrives for every raw Address/Age; an unfetched key outside [58,326) cannot affect the output | **proved** | ROBUSTNESS §6: slot-word circular differences exclude all comb-target differences, including unused word patterns. `Report/proofs/check_front_arrivals.py` checks all 524,288 Address/position/direction cases with actual gates; `figs/robustness/front_arrival_certificate.json`. This is a local lemma, not noisy simulation. |
| D1e | From Enc or Enc_a of any z, separated E0-only noise gives decode(X_(nU))=F^n(z) for every n | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §6, (5b), strengthens D1c to all E0-only overwrite values/patterns. The whole boundary state is Enc_a(F^n(z)) except current faults, closing the period induction. Whole boundary equality holds outside current errors. A common arbitrary legal Age restores some admissible encoding in less than 2U. It does not restore an intended bit whose initial majority was wrong, prove geometric recovery, localize an upper footprint or decode partial geometry. `figs/robustness/aligned_restoration.json` is supplementary finite support. |
| D1g | The restricted J16 seed at initial good clock zero has a complete localized first-period simulation | **proved conditionally** | ROBUSTNESS §12, (J17), and C1v/G9. If initially encoded outside I, that decoded change is confined to at most two adjacent upper cells. Two initial encoding levels give exact clean-boundary recovery via one-step upper-pair correction. This is not a damaged-trajectory simulation theorem for arbitrary seeds/times or continuing E0. |

Conditional rounding can reduce separation to (22,23), outside the literal (24,24) definition; ROBUSTNESS §9. |

## What would raise a status

The proof obligations are spelled out in ROBUSTNESS §§6–11. The necessary
remaining work is extension of the proved healthy copy/clock invariant to
damaged trajectories, a mixed-noise structural repair lemma at Q=384, a complete
all-field wipe/provenance and effective-error argument, and a quantified
all-level island induction (including Flag2 for G17, or a replacement for
SP3/SP4's changed mechanism). SP3 now has complete scoped arbitrary-island
theorems without further noise through width196 and amid E0 through width133;
the width141 mixed-E0 extension and command-window hypotheses are not
proved at Q384. SP4 has width160 mixed-E0 repair at Q448 and full
second-boundary recovery of one first-period real level1 error at any
core tick with two initial encodings. J35/J36 extend one real set to any
pristine-orbit work period and pin a one-tick computed-boundary upper pair
map. The raw one-tick version was false at a fresh boundary overwrite and
is withdrawn (G20l). J37 proves prospective (23,24) packing, and J38/J39
prove the simultaneous computed-boundary map for E1-only noise on the
pristine infinite hierarchy. The extension to upper inputs already damaged
by higher errors and the higher-island induction remain unproved. J42
provides an aligned two-level operational barrier with E0/density faults
on the right; right-side real level1 sets and left erosion remain outside
its scope.
J43/J46 permit one right real core separated by a stated881 support
margin and give two-period far-domain recovery, with a separate
all-phase boundary/capture/command induction at the shorter margin.
J44 gives a finite two-level barrier after sacrificing the first2 lower
colonies, and contains a real core whose nonsparse kernel lies strictly
before that shifted boundary. It does not restore that pair or tolerate
another real core on the protected side. J47 combines the one-core
cases into two-period containment for any right position with an
unrestricted26-cell upper boundary strip. It does not restore the
operational snapshot needed to apply the argument to a later core.
Repeated damaged-input induction and the higher-island eroder remain
unproved. J45's broader arbitrary-data premise is still restricted to
the early inactive geometry stage.
J48 refutes the naive discarded-prefix plus arbitrary-new-pair closure;
a successful extension must prove reachable-word restrictions, earlier
prefix restoration, or a larger repaired envelope. The anchor11 density
fact alone does not supply that extension.
Merely running deeper rings or measuring
another wave speed would add evidence, not prove these obligations.
